\Delta_{\Phi}=\max(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{0}),\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{1}))-\min_{\boldsymbol{x}}\Phi(\boldsymbol{x})
) :

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{i})\|^{2}]&\leq\frac{2\Delta_{\Phi}}{\eta_{x}}+3\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{0}\|^{2}\\
&\quad-(1-2\kappa\ell\eta_{x}-3\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2})\sum_{i=1}^{T-2}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i}\|^{2}]\\
&\quad+3\ell^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{i}-\boldsymbol{y}_{i}\|^{2}]+3\ell^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{i}-\boldsymbol{y}_{i-1}\|^{2}]+30\frac{(T-1)\sigma^{2}}{M_{x}}\\
\end{split}

(46)

We can replace
\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{i}^{*}-\boldsymbol{y}_{i}\|^{2}
with its upper bound obtained in Lemma
A.5
to get:

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{i})\|^{2}&\leq\frac{2\Delta_{\Phi}}{\eta_{x}}+3\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{27}{7}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{1}\|^{2}\\
&\quad-(1-2\kappa\ell\eta_{x}-3\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}-\frac{54}{7}\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{2}\ell^{2})\sum_{i=1}^{T-2}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i}\|^{2}]\\
&\quad+\frac{108}{7}\ell^{2}\sum_{i=2}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{z}_{i}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{i-1}\|^{2}]+3\ell^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{i}-\boldsymbol{y}_{i-1}\|^{2}]+30\frac{(T-1)\sigma^{2}}{M_{x}}\\
&\quad+\frac{6}{7}\frac{(T-2)\sigma^{2}}{M_{y}}\end{split}

(47)

Now note that
\frac{108}{7}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{z}_{i+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{i}\|^{2}]+3\sum_{i=2}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{i}-\boldsymbol{y}_{i-1}\|^{2}]\leq 15.5\mathbb{E}[\boldsymbol{r}_{i}]
. Therefore we have:

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{i})\|^{2}&\leq\frac{2\Delta_{\Phi}}{\eta_{x}}+3\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{27}{7}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{1}\|^{2}\\
&\quad-(1-2\kappa\ell\eta_{x}-3\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}-\frac{54}{7}\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{2}\ell^{2})\sum_{i=1}^{T-2}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i}\|^{2}]\\
&\quad+15.5\ell^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\boldsymbol{r}_{i}]+30\frac{(T-1)\sigma^{2}}{M_{x}}+\frac{6}{7}\frac{(T-2)\sigma^{2}}{M_{y}}\\
\end{split}

(48)

Furthermore, using Lemma
A.6
, we can find an upper bound on
\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\boldsymbol{r}_{i}]
, and replacing it in above equation yields:

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{i})\|^{2}&\leq\frac{2\Delta_{\Phi}}{\eta_{x}}+186\kappa\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}[\boldsymbol{r}_{1}]+11\kappa\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{0}\|^{2}+3\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{27}{7}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{1}\|^{2}\\
&\quad-(1-2\kappa\ell\eta_{x}-3\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}-\frac{54}{7}\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{2}\ell^{2}-2248\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{4}\ell^{2})\sum_{i=1}^{T-2}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i}\|^{2}]\\
&\quad+\frac{62\kappa\sigma^{2}(T-2)}{M_{y}}+30\frac{(T-1)\sigma^{2}}{M_{x}}+\frac{6}{7}\frac{(T-2)\sigma^{2}}{M_{y}}\\
\end{split}

(49)

By letting
\eta_{x}=\frac{1}{50\kappa^{2}\ell}
, it holds that
-(1-2\kappa\ell\eta_{x}-3\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}-\frac{54}{7}\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{2}\ell^{2}-2248\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{4}\ell^{2})\sum_{i=1}^{T-2}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{i}\|^{2}]\leq 0
. Therefore, with the choice of letting rate
\eta_{x}=\frac{1}{50\kappa^{2}\ell}
and simplifying the terms, we have:

\begin{split}\frac{1}{T-1}\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{i})\|^{2}]&\leq 100\frac{\kappa^{2}\ell\Delta_{\Phi}}{T-1}+186\frac{\kappa\ell^{2}}{T-1}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{1}+\eta_{y}(\boldsymbol{g}_{y,1}-\boldsymbol{g}_{y,0})\|^{2}\\
&\quad+47\frac{\kappa\ell^{2}}{T-1}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{0}\|^{2}+14\frac{\kappa\ell^{2}}{T-1}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{0}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\frac{27}{7}\frac{\ell^{2}}{T-1}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{1}\|^{2}+\frac{63\kappa\sigma^{2}}{M_{y}}+30\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M_{x}}\end{split}

(50)

Using Young’s inequality and
\ell
-smoothness of
f
, we have:

\begin{split}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{1}+\eta_{y}(\boldsymbol{g}_{y,1}-\boldsymbol{g}_{y,0})\|^{2}\leq 2\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{18}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{0}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{18}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{0}\|^{2}\end{split}

(51)

Plugging this into Equation
50
, we have:

\begin{split}\frac{1}{T-1}\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{i})\|^{2}]&\leq 100\frac{\kappa^{2}\ell\Delta_{\Phi}}{T-1}+376\frac{\kappa\ell^{2}}{T-1}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{1}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+58\frac{\kappa\ell^{2}}{T-1}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{0}\|^{2}+25\frac{\kappa\ell^{2}}{T-1}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{0}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\frac{63\kappa\sigma^{2}}{M_{y}}+30\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M_{x}}\end{split}

(52)

Now by letting
M_{x}=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}
,
M_{y}=\frac{\kappa\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}
and
D_{0}=\max(\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{1}\|^{2},\|\boldsymbol{x}_{1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{0}\|^{2},\|\boldsymbol{y}_{1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{0}\|^{2})
, we have:

\frac{1}{T-1}\sum_{i=1}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{i})\|^{2}]\leq O(\frac{\kappa^{2}\ell\Delta_{\Phi}+\kappa\ell^{2}D_{0}}{T-1})+O(\epsilon^{2})

(53)

which completes the proof as stated.
∎

A.2
Proof of Convergence of EG

In this section, we present the convergence proof of the EG algorithm as detailed in Algorithm
3
. We start by providing the proof sketch.

Algorithm 3

(Stochastic) EG

Input :

Initialization
(\boldsymbol{x}_{-1}=\boldsymbol{x}_{0},\boldsymbol{y}_{-1}=\boldsymbol{y}_{0})
, learning rates
\eta_{x},\eta_{y}

for

t=1,2,\dots,T

do

\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1/2}=\boldsymbol{x}_{t}-\eta_{x}\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})
;
\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}=\boldsymbol{y}_{t}+\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})

\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1}=\boldsymbol{x}_{t}-\eta_{x}\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1/2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2})
;
\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}=\boldsymbol{y}_{t}+\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1/2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2})
; #EG

\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1/2}=\boldsymbol{x}_{t}-\eta_{x}\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}
;
\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}=\boldsymbol{y}_{t}+\eta_{y}\boldsymbol{g}_{y,t}
;

\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1}=\boldsymbol{x}_{t}-\eta_{x}\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t+1/2}
;
\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}=\boldsymbol{y}_{t}+\eta_{y}\boldsymbol{g}_{y,t+1/2}
; # Stochastic EG

end for

Proof sketch.

We highlight the key ideas here. The first step is to derive to find an upper bound on
\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1})-\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})
. Using
\kappa\ell
-smoothness property of
\Phi(\boldsymbol{x})
at point
\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1}
, and
\boldsymbol{x}_{t}
we bound the
\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1})-\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})
term, and then taking summation over all iterates, we derive the following primal descent lemma:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{T})]-\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{0})&\leq-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}]-\frac{\eta_{x}}{4}(1-O(\eta_{x}))\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}]\\
&\quad+O(\eta_{x}\ell^{2})\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}]O(\eta_{x})\frac{\sigma^{2}T}{M}.\end{split}

(54)

We also show the following dual descent lemma to directly bound
\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}
term in above inequality:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1}\|^{2}]&\leq(1-\frac{1}{12\kappa})\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}]+O(\kappa^{3}\eta_{x}^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}]+\frac{2\sigma^{2}}{M\ell^{2}}\end{split}

where we assumed
\eta_{y}=\frac{1}{4\ell}
. Combining the primal and dual descent lemmas yields the desired result on the convergence of EG to an
\epsilon
-stationary point.

In what follows, we provide the formal key lemmas, and the complete proof of Theorem
4.2
, and Theorem
4.4
for EG algorithm. Similar to OGDA, for the sake of brevity, we only present the convergence proof for stochastic version of EG (Theorem
4.4
), since by letting
\sigma=0
, we can recover the proof for deterministic algorithm (Theorem
4.2
).

Lemma A.7
.

Let
\eta_{y}=\frac{1}{4\ell}
, and
M=\max(M_{x},M_{y})
. Also assume
\eta_{x}\leq\frac{1}{64\kappa^{2}\ell}
, then the iterates of Algorithm
3
satisfy the following inequalities:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1}\|^{2}]&\leq(1-\frac{1}{12\kappa})\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}]+18\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{3}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}]+2\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M\ell^{2}}\end{split}

(55)

\sum_{i=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{i}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{i}\|^{2}]\leq 12\kappa\|\boldsymbol{y}_{0}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{0}\|^{2}+216\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{4}\sum_{i=0}^{T-2}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,i}\|^{2}]+\frac{24\kappa\sigma^{2}(T-1)}{M\ell^{2}}

(56)

Proof of Lemma
A.7
.

Now we turn to convergence analysis for EG. The deterministic and stochastic variants of the EG algorithm are detailed in Algorithm
3
.

To prove this lemma, we built on top of analysis in
[
39
]
. We start by noting that:

\begin{split}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}&=\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
&\quad-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
&\quad-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{g}_{y,t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}\rangle\\
&\quad+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{g}_{y,t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\rangle\\
\end{split}

(57)

Let
\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{i}=\boldsymbol{g}_{y,i}-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{i},\boldsymbol{y}_{i})
. We have:

\begin{split}&2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{g}_{y,t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}\rangle+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{g}_{y,t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\rangle\\
&=2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{g}_{y,t}-\boldsymbol{g}_{y,t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}\rangle+2\eta_{y}\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1/2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}),\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\rangle\\
&\quad+\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\rangle\\
&\leq\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}\|^{2}+\eta_{y}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{y,t}-\boldsymbol{g}_{y,t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}-2\eta_{y}\mu\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\rangle\\
&\leq\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}\|^{2}+2\eta_{y}^{2}\|\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}})\|^{2}-2\eta_{y}\mu\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\rangle+4\eta_{y}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta_{y}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
&\leq\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}\|^{2}+2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}+2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}-2\eta_{y}\mu\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\rangle+4\eta_{y}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta_{y}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\end{split}

(58)

where in the first inequality, we used
\mu
-strong-concavity of
f(\boldsymbol{x},.)
, and in the second inequality, we used Young’s inequality, and in the last one, we used the smoothness property. Now plugging Equation
58
back to Equation
57
, we have:

\begin{split}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\|^{2}&\leq\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\|^{2}-(1-2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}-2\eta_{y}\mu\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\rangle+4\eta_{y}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta_{y}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\end{split}

(59)

Using Young’s inequality, we can rewrite Equation
59
as follows:

\begin{split}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\|^{2}&\leq(1-\eta_{y}\mu)\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\|^{2}-(1-2\eta_{y}\mu-2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}+\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\rangle+4\eta_{y}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+4\eta_{y}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\end{split}

(60)

Assuming
\eta_{y}=\frac{1}{4\ell}
, using Young’s inequality, we have the following equation:

\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\|^{2}\leq(1+\frac{1}{16\kappa})\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+(1+16\kappa)\|\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}

(61)

\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1}\|^{2}\leq(1+\frac{1}{16\kappa})\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\|^{2}+(1+16\kappa)\|\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\|^{2}

(62)

Combining Equations
60
,
61
,
62
and using the
\kappa
Lipschitzness of
\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(.)
, and noting that
1-2\eta_{y}\mu-2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}>0
, we get:

\begin{split}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1}\|^{2}&\leq(1-\frac{1}{8\kappa})\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+17\kappa^{3}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}+17\kappa^{3}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1/2}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+2\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1/2}\rangle+\frac{1}{2\ell^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2\ell^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\end{split}

(63)

Using Young’s inequality, we have:

\begin{split}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}&=\eta_{x}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}\\
&\leq 2\eta_{x}^{2}\|\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\|^{2}+4\eta_{x}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}+4\eta_{x}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\leq 2\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}+2\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta_{x}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}+4\eta_{x}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\leq 2\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+4\eta_{x}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}+4\eta_{x}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\leq 2\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}+8\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta_{x}^{2}}{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta_{x}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+4\eta_{x}^{2}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t}\|^{2}+2\eta_{x}^{2}\ell\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\rangle\end{split}

(64)

where in the last inequality, we used Lemma
A.3
. Plugging Equation
64
, in Equation
63
, and assuming
\eta_{x}\leq\frac{1}{64\kappa^{2}\ell}
gives:

\begin{split}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1}\|^{2}&\leq(1-\frac{1}{12\kappa})\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+18\kappa^{3}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+2\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1/2}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\rangle+\frac{1}{64\kappa\ell}\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\rangle\\
&\quad+\frac{1}{\ell^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2\ell^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{4\ell^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{4\ell^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t}\|^{2}\end{split}

(65)

or equivalently:

\begin{split}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1}\|^{2}&\leq(1-\frac{1}{12\kappa})\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+18\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{3}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+2\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\rangle+\frac{1}{64\kappa\ell}\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\rangle\\
&\quad+\frac{1}{\ell^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2\ell^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{4\ell^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{4\ell^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t}\|^{2}\end{split}

(66)

Taking expectation from both sides of Equation
66
yields:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t+1}\|^{2}]&\leq\left(1-\frac{1}{12\kappa}\right)\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}]+18\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{3}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}]+2\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M\ell^{2}}\end{split}

(67)

Now using Lemma
A.2
we get

\sum_{i=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{i}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{i}\|^{2}]\leq 12\kappa\|\boldsymbol{y}_{0}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{0}\|^{2}+216\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{4}\sum_{i=0}^{T-2}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,i}\|^{2}]+\frac{24\kappa\sigma^{2}(T-1)}{M\ell^{2}}

(68)

as stated in the lemma.
∎

Lemma A.8
.

Let
\Phi(\boldsymbol{x})=\max_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x},\boldsymbol{y})
, and
\eta_{y}=\frac{1}{4\ell}
. Then the iterates of Algorithm
3
satisfy the following inequality:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1})]&\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})]-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}]-\frac{\eta_{x}}{4}(1-2\eta_{x}\kappa\ell-8\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}]\\
&\quad+5\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}]+7\eta_{x}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\end{split}

(69)

Proof of Lemma
A.8
.

Let
\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{i}=\boldsymbol{g}_{x,i}-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{i},\boldsymbol{y}_{i})
.Using smoothness property at
\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1}
and
\boldsymbol{x}_{t}
, we have:

\begin{split}\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1})&\leq\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-\eta_{x}\langle\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t}),\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t+\frac{1}{2}}\rangle+\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa\ell\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
&=\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}(1-2\eta_{x}\kappa\ell)\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
&\leq\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}(1-2\eta_{x}\kappa\ell)\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\eta_{x}\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}})\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
&\leq\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}(1-2\eta_{x}\kappa\ell)\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
\end{split}

(70)

Using Young’s inequality, we have:

\begin{split}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}\end{split}

(71)

Plugging Equation
71
back to Equation
70
, and assuming
\eta_{x}\leq\frac{1}{2\kappa\ell}
results in:

\begin{split}\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1})&\leq\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}-\frac{\eta_{x}}{4}(1-2\eta_{x}\kappa\ell)\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
&\leq\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}-\frac{\eta_{x}}{4}(1-2\eta_{x}\kappa\ell)\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\|\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\|^{2}\\
&\quad+2\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}+2\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t}\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
&\leq\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}-\frac{\eta_{x}}{4}(1-2\eta_{x}\kappa\ell)\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+2\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}+2\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t}\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
\end{split}

(72)

Using Lemma
A.3
and Young’s inequality, we have:

\begin{split}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}&\leq 3\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+2\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\leq 5\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{8\ell^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{2\ell}\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\rangle\end{split}

(73)

Plugging Equation
73
in Equation
72
, we get:

\begin{split}\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1})&\leq\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}-\frac{\eta_{x}}{4}(1-2\eta_{x}\kappa\ell-8\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2})\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}+5\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+\frac{3}{8}\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{2}\eta_{x}\ell\langle\boldsymbol{\delta}^{y}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\rangle+2\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}+2\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t}\|^{2}+\eta_{x}\|\boldsymbol{\delta}^{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}}\|^{2}\\
\end{split}

(74)

Taking expectations from both sides of Equation
74
, we have:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+1})]&\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})]-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}]-\frac{\eta_{x}}{4}(1-2\eta_{x}\kappa\ell-8\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2})\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}]\\
&\quad+5\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}]+7\eta_{x}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{M}\end{split}

(75)

∎

Proof of Theorem
4.2
, and Theorem
4.4
for EG.

Equipped with the above lemmas, we can prove the theorem as follows. We start by taking summation from
t=0
to
t=T-1
of Equation
69
in Lemma
A.8
, to get:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{T})]&\leq\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{0})-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}]-\frac{\eta_{x}}{4}(1-2\eta_{x}\kappa\ell-8\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2})\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}]\\
&\quad+5\eta_{x}\ell^{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}]+7\eta_{x}\frac{\sigma^{2}T}{M}\end{split}

(76)

Replacing
\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{t}\|^{2}]
with the upper bound in Lemma
A.7
, we have:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{T})]&\leq 60\eta_{x}\kappa\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{0}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{0}\|^{2}+\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{0})-\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}]\\
&-\frac{\eta_{x}}{4}(1-2\eta_{x}\kappa\ell-8\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}-4320\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{4}\ell^{2})\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\boldsymbol{g}_{x,t}\|^{2}]\\
&+\frac{120\eta_{x}\kappa\sigma^{2}(T-1)}{M}+7\eta_{x}\frac{\sigma^{2}T}{M}\end{split}

(77)

Let
\eta_{x}=\frac{1}{75\kappa^{2}\ell}
. Then
1-2\eta_{x}\kappa\ell-8\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}-4320\eta_{x}^{2}\kappa^{4}\ell^{2}>0
. After rearranging and simplifying the terms of Equation
77
, we have:

\begin{split}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}]&\leq\frac{2\Delta_{\Phi}}{\eta_{x}}+120\kappa\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{0}-\boldsymbol{y}_{0}^{*}\|^{2}+\frac{240\kappa\sigma^{2}T}{M}+\frac{14\sigma^{2}T}{M}\end{split}

(78)

Replacing
\eta_{x}=\frac{1}{75\kappa^{2}\ell}
in Equation
78
, we have:

\begin{split}\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}]&\leq\frac{150\kappa^{2}\ell\Delta_{\Phi}+120\kappa\ell^{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{0}-\boldsymbol{y}_{0}^{*}\|^{2}}{T}+\frac{240\kappa\sigma^{2}}{M}+\frac{14\sigma^{2}}{M}.\end{split}

(79)

Now by letting,
M=\frac{\kappa\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}
, and
D_{0}=\|\boldsymbol{y}_{0}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}_{0}\|^{2}
, we have:

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\|\nabla\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}]\leq O(\frac{\kappa^{2}\ell\Delta_{\Phi}+\kappa\ell^{2}D_{0}}{T})+O(\epsilon^{2})

(80)

∎

A.3
Tightness Analysis

In this section we provide the complete proofs for Theorem
4.5
(Subsection
A.3.1
), and Theorem
4.6
(Subsection
A.3.2
), showing the tightness of the obtained upper bounds given our choice of learning rates.

A.3.1
GDA

Proof of Theorem
4.5
.

Recall that we consider the following quadratic NC-SC function
f:\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}

\displaystyle f(x,y):=-\tfrac{1}{4}\ell x^{2}+bxy-\tfrac{1}{2}\mu y^{2}.

We know
f
is nonconvex in
x
(it is actually concave in
x
) and
\mu
strongly concave in
y
. Assume
\kappa:=\ell/\mu\geq 4
and choose
b=\sqrt{\mu(\ell+2\mu_{x})/2}
for some
0<\mu_{x}\leq\ell/2
to be chosen later.
Then we know
b\leq\ell/2
and it is easy to verify
f
is
\ell
smooth. Note that the primal function

\displaystyle\Phi(x)=\max_{y}f(x,y)=\tfrac{1}{2}\mu_{x}x^{2}

is actually strongly convex. This also justifies the symbol for
\mu_{x}
. We use GDA to find the solution for
\min_{x}\max_{y}f(x,y)
. Actually for this problem the optimal solution is achieved at the origin. The stepsizes are chosen as

\eta_{x}=\frac{c_{1}}{\kappa^{2}\ell}
and
\eta_{y}=\frac{c_{2}}{\ell}
for some small enough numerical constants
c_{1}
and
c_{2}
such that
c=c_{2}/c_{1}\geq 1
. Also denote
r=\eta_{y}/\eta_{x}=c\kappa^{2}
as the stepsize ratio. Then the GDA update rule can be written as

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}x_{k+1}\\
y_{k+1}\end{pmatrix}=(I+\eta_{x}\mathbf{M})\cdot\begin{pmatrix}x_{k}\\
y_{k}\end{pmatrix},

(81)

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{M}:=\begin{pmatrix}\ell/2&-b\\
rb&-\mu r\end{pmatrix}.

We note that the above update is a linear time invariant system. We need to analyze its eigenvalues. Let
\lambda_{1}
and
\lambda_{2}
be the two eigenvalues of
\mathbf{M}
, we have

\displaystyle\lambda_{1,2}=-\frac{1}{2}\left(\mu r-\frac{1}{2}\ell\right)\pm\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\left(\mu r-\frac{1}{2}\ell\right)^{2}-4r\mu\mu_{x}}.

Note that if we choose
\mu_{x}<\ell/8
, plugging into
r=c\kappa^{2}
, we can bound

\displaystyle 0\geq\lambda_{1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{(2c\kappa-1)\ell}{4}\left(1-\sqrt{1-\frac{4c\kappa\mu_{x}}{(c\kappa-1/2)^{2}\ell}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{2c\kappa\mu_{x}}{c\kappa-1/2}\geq-4\mu_{x}.

Let
s_{1}
be the corresponding eigenvalue of
I+\eta_{x}M
, for small enough
c_{1}\leq 1
, it satisfies

\displaystyle 0\leq 1-\frac{4c_{1}\mu_{x}}{\kappa^{2}}\leq s_{1}=1+\eta_{x}\lambda_{1}\leq 1.

We adversarially choose the initial point
(x_{0},y_{0})
such that it is parallel to the eigenvector of
I+\eta_{x}M
corresponding to
s_{1}
. We can always choose
x_{0}\geq 0
for simplicity. Then we have

\displaystyle\begin{pmatrix}x_{k+1}\\
y_{k+1}\end{pmatrix}

\displaystyle=(I+\eta_{x}\mathbf{M})^{T}\begin{pmatrix}x_{0}\\
y_{0}\end{pmatrix}=s_{1}^{T}\begin{pmatrix}x_{0}\\
y_{0}\end{pmatrix},

so we can compute the magnitude of
x_{T}
as
x_{T}=s_{1}^{T}x_{0}
. Also note that
\Delta_{\Phi}=\Phi(x_{0})=\frac{1}{2}\mu_{x}x_{0}^{2}
. Note that if
\Delta_{\Phi}=0
, this lemma is trivially true. Therefore we can assume
\Delta_{\Phi}>0
. Choosing
\mu_{x}=\epsilon^{2}/\Delta_{\Phi}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\nabla\Phi(\bar{x})\right|=\mu_{x}\bar{x}\geq

\displaystyle\mu_{x}x_{T}\geq\mu_{x}x_{0}\left(1-\frac{4c_{1}\mu_{x}}{\kappa^{2}}\right)^{T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2}\epsilon\left(1-\frac{4c_{1}\epsilon^{2}}{\kappa^{2}\Delta_{\Phi}}\right)^{T},

where
\bar{x}\geq x_{T}
because
x_{0}\geq x_{1}\geq\cdots\geq x_{T}
and
\bar{x}
is sampled from this sequence.
Then we know that to achieve
\left|\nabla\Phi(\bar{x})\right|\leq\epsilon
, we must have

T=\Omega\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\Delta_{\Phi}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right)
as stated.

∎

A.3.2
EG/OGDA

Proof of Theorem
4.6
for EG.

We consider the same quadratic hard example
f
and notation used in the proof of Theorem
4.5
.
For simplicity, denote
\boldsymbol{w}=(x,y)
. Then EG satisfies

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{w}_{k+1/2}=

\displaystyle(I+\eta_{x}\mathbf{M})\boldsymbol{w}_{k},

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{w}_{k+1}=

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{w}_{k}+\eta_{x}\mathbf{M}\boldsymbol{w}_{k+1/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(I+\eta_{x}\mathbf{M}+\eta_{x}^{2}\mathbf{M}^{2})\boldsymbol{w}_{k}.

Therefore, similar to GDA, EG is also a linear time invariant system. The transition matrix for EG is
(I+\eta_{x}\mathbf{M}+\eta_{x}^{2}\mathbf{M}^{2})
.
Its eigenvalues are

\displaystyle s_{i}=1+\eta_{x}\lambda_{i}+\eta_{x}^{2}\lambda_{i}^{2}\geq 1+\eta_{x}\lambda_{i},\quad i=1,2.

The rest of analysis is the same as that of GDA.

∎

Proof of Theorem
4.6
for OGDA.

We consider the same quadratic hard example
f
and the notation used in the proofs of Theorems
5.1
and
5.2
.
The dynamics of OGDA is

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{w}_{k+1}=\boldsymbol{w}_{k}+2\eta_{x}\mathbf{M}\boldsymbol{w}_{k}-\eta_{x}\mathbf{M}\boldsymbol{w}_{k-1}.

If we initialize
\boldsymbol{w}_{0}
parallel to the eigenvector of
\mathbf{M}
corresponding to
\lambda_{1}
and let
\boldsymbol{w}_{1}=\boldsymbol{w}_{0}
, we know every
\boldsymbol{w}_{k}
is parallel to it, i.e.,
\boldsymbol{w}_{k}=z_{k}\boldsymbol{w}_{0}
for some scalar
z_{k}
which satisfies

\displaystyle z_{k+1}=z_{k}+2\eta_{x}\lambda_{1}z_{k}-\eta_{x}\lambda_{1}z_{k-1}.

The general solution of the above recurrence relation is

\displaystyle z_{k}=a\alpha^{k}+b\beta^{k}

for some constant
a,b
and

\displaystyle\alpha=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(1+2\eta_{x}\lambda_{1}+\sqrt{1+4\eta_{x}^{2}\lambda_{1}^{2}}\right),

\displaystyle\beta=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(1+2\eta_{x}\lambda_{1}-\sqrt{1+4\eta_{x}^{2}\lambda_{1}^{2}}\right).

We have

\displaystyle 1+\eta_{x}\lambda_{1}\leq\alpha\leq 1,\quad\eta_{x}\lambda_{1}\leq\beta\leq 0.

Using the initial condition
z_{-1}=z_{0}=1
, we can get the constants

\displaystyle a=

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha(1-\beta)}{\alpha-\beta}=\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{1+4\eta_{x}^{2}\lambda_{1}^{2}}}\geq 1/2,

\displaystyle b=

\displaystyle-\frac{\beta(1-\alpha)}{\alpha-\beta}=\frac{\sqrt{1+4\eta_{x}^{2}\lambda_{1}^{2}}-1}{2\sqrt{1+4\eta_{x}^{2}\lambda_{1}^{2}}}\leq\eta_{x}^{2}\lambda_{1}^{2}.

We can bound

\displaystyle|z_{T}|

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\left(1+\eta_{x}\lambda_{2}\right)^{T}-|\eta_{x}\lambda_{1}|^{k+2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\frac{4c_{1}\mu_{x}}{\kappa^{2}}\right)^{T}-\frac{1}{4},

where we use the fact
|\eta_{x}\lambda_{1}|\leq 1/2
.
Similar to the analysis for GDA, choosing
\mu_{x}=50\epsilon^{2}/\Delta_{\Phi}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\nabla\Phi(\bar{x})\right|=\mu_{x}\bar{x}\geq

\displaystyle\mu_{x}x_{T}\geq\mu_{x}x_{0}\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\frac{4c_{1}\mu_{x}}{\kappa^{2}}\right)^{T}-\frac{1}{4}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 10\epsilon\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\frac{4c_{1}\mu_{x}}{\kappa^{2}}\right)^{T}-\frac{1}{4}\right].

Therefore, if
\left|\nabla\Phi(\bar{x})\right|\leq\epsilon
, we must have

\displaystyle T=\Omega\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\mu_{x}}\right)=\Omega\left(\frac{\kappa^{2}\Delta_{\Phi}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right).

∎

Appendix B
Proof of Convergence in Nonconvex-Concave Setting

B.1
Proof of convergence of OGDA

In this section, the convergence of OGDA in NC-C setting has been established. Before presenting the complete proofs, here we briefly discuss the proof sketch.

Proof sketch

We start from the standard descent analysis on Moreau envelope function
[
9
]
. Let
\delta_{t}=\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})
, then we can show:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Phi_{1/2\ell}({\boldsymbol{x}}_{0})-\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{T+1})}{T+1}+O\left(\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\ell\delta_{t}\right)+O(\ell\eta_{x}^{2}G^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}O(\|\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\|^{2}).

It turns out that the gradient norm depends on two terms, difference between gradient at time
t
and
t-1
and
\delta_{t}
: primal function gap at iteration
t
. To bound the first term, we can utilize smoothness of
\nabla f
and reduce the problem to bounding
\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}
:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=0}^{T}O\left(\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\sum_{j=0}^{T}(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{j}\right)\delta_{t}+\sum_{t=0}^{T}O\left(\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}G^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{T}(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{j}\right).

Here we reduce difference between dual iterates to primal function gap
\delta_{t}
. Now, it remains to bound
\delta_{t}
. We have the following recursion relation holding for any
t
and any
s\leq t
:

\displaystyle\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\leq O(\eta_{x}(t-s)G^{2})+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}(\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-y^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\|^{2}+\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}\ell G^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2})+\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}),\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}),\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\rangle.

(82)

If we let
s
stay the same for some iterations,
({1}/{T+1})\sum_{t=0}^{T}\delta_{t}
vanishes in a telescoping fashion.

In the following, we present the key lemmas, and complete convergence proof of OGDA. First let us introduce some useful lemmas for deterministic setting.

B.1.1
Useful Lemmas

Lemma B.1
.

For OGDA (Algorithm
2
), under Theorem
4.9
’s assumptions, the following statement holds for the generated sequence
\{\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\}
during algorithm proceeding and any
\boldsymbol{y}\in\mathcal{Y}
:

\begin{split}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}\|^{2}&\leq\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\|^{2}+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle\\
&\quad+\eta_{y}\eta_{x}^{2}\ell G^{2}-2\eta_{y}\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}),\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}\rangle\\
&\quad+2\eta_{y}\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}),\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}\rangle.\end{split}

(83)

Proof.

According to updating rule of
\boldsymbol{y}
:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}=\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}+2\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\right).

Following the analysis in
[
40
]
, we let
\varepsilon_{t-1}=\eta_{y}(\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}))-\eta_{y}(\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}))
and re-write the updating rule as:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}=\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}+\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\varepsilon_{t-1}\right)

Then, due to the property of projection onto convex set we have the following inequality that holds for any
\boldsymbol{y}\in\mathcal{Y}
:

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t})^{\top}(\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})+\varepsilon_{t-1})\geq 0.

Using the identity that
\langle\boldsymbol{a},\boldsymbol{b}\rangle=\frac{1}{2}(\|\boldsymbol{a}+\boldsymbol{b}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{a}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{b}\|^{2})
we have:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})+\varepsilon_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})+\varepsilon_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+2\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle-2\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\varepsilon_{t-1}\rangle

Now we plug the definition of
\varepsilon_{t-1}
into above inequality to get:

\begin{split}\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}&\leq\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle\\
&\quad-2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},(\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}))\rangle\\
&\quad+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\rangle\\
&\leq\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle\\
&\quad-2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},(\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}))\rangle\\
&\quad+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\rangle\\
&\quad+\eta_{y}\ell(\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2}\|^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\|^{2})\\
&\leq\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\|^{2}+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle\\
&-2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},(\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}))\rangle\\
&\quad+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\rangle+\eta_{y}\eta_{x}^{2}\ell G^{2},\end{split}

(84)

which concludes the proof.

∎

Lemma B.2
.

For OGDA (Algorithm
2
), under the same assumptions made as in Theorem
4.8
, the following statement holds for the generated sequence
\{\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\},\{\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\}
during algorithm proceeding:

\displaystyle\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\Phi_{1/2\ell}({\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1})+2\eta_{x}\ell\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\right)-\frac{\eta_{x}}{8}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})\|^{2}+3\ell\eta_{x}^{2}G^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\|\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\|^{2}.

Proof.

Let
\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}=\arg\min_{\boldsymbol{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\Phi(\boldsymbol{x})+\ell\|\boldsymbol{x}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\|^{2}
. Notice that:

\begin{split}\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})&\leq\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1})+\ell\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}\\
&\leq\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1})+\ell(\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\|^{2}\\
&\quad+2\eta_{x}\langle 2\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}),\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\rangle+3\eta_{x}^{2}G^{2})\end{split}

According to smoothness of
f(\cdot,\boldsymbol{y})
, we have:

\displaystyle\langle\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq f(\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})+\frac{\ell}{2}\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\frac{\ell}{2}\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\|^{2}.

So we have

\displaystyle\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1})+\ell\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1})+\ell\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}-\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\eta_{x}\ell\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\frac{\ell}{2}\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\|^{2}\right)+3\ell\eta_{x}^{2}G^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta_{x}\ell\left(\frac{1}{2\ell}\|\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\|^{2}+\frac{\ell}{2}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}-\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\Phi_{1/2\ell}({\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1})+2\eta_{x}\ell\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\right)-\frac{\eta_{x}\ell^{2}}{2}\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\|^{2}+3\ell\eta_{x}^{2}G^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\|\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\|^{2}.

Using the fact that
\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\|=\frac{1}{2\ell}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})\|
will conclude the proof.

∎

Lemma B.3

(Iterates gap)
.

For OGDA (Algorithm
2
), under Theorem
4.8
’s assumptions, the following statement holds for the generated sequence
\{\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\}
during algorithm proceeding:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{j}\right)4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{j}\right)2\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}G^{2}.

Proof.

Observe that

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\eta_{y}^{2}\left\|2\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta_{y}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\right\|^{2}+2\eta_{y}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\right)+2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\left(\left\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\left\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\right\|^{2}+4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\right)+2\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}G^{2}.

Unrolling the recursion yields:

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{t-1}\left\|\boldsymbol{y}_{0}-\boldsymbol{y}_{-1}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{t}(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{t-j}4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{j-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{j-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{j-1})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{t}(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{t-j}2\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}G^{2}.

Since
\boldsymbol{y}_{0}=\boldsymbol{y}_{-1}
, we have:

\displaystyle\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{t}(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{t-j}4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{j-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{j-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{j-1})\right)+\sum_{j=1}^{t}(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{t-j}2\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}G^{2}.

Finally, summing the above inequality over
t=0
to
T
yields:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{j}\right)4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{j}\right)2\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}G^{2}.

∎

Lemma B.4
.

For OGDA (Algorithm
2
), under Theorem
4.8
’s assumptions, the following statement holds for the generated sequence
\{\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\}
during algorithm proceeding and
\forall s\leq t
:

\displaystyle\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta_{x}(t-s)G^{2}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}\left(\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-y^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(x_{s})\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\|^{2}+\eta_{y}\eta_{x}^{2}\ell G^{2}\right)-\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}),

\displaystyle\quad\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\rangle+\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}),\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\rangle.

Proof.

Observe that:

\displaystyle\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t}))-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t}))+f(\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s}))-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s}))

\displaystyle\quad+f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s}))-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq 2(t-s)\eta_{x}G^{2}-\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle,

where in the last step we use the concavity of
f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\cdot)
.

Plugging in Lemma
B.1
will conclude the proof as follows:

\displaystyle\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})

\displaystyle\leq 2(t-s)\eta_{x}G^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}\left(\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}\eta_{y}\eta_{x}^{2}\ell G^{2}-\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}),\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}),\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}\rangle.

∎

Lemma B.5
.

For OGDA (Algorithm
2
), under the same assumptions made in Theorem
4.8
, the following statement holds for the generated sequence
\{\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\},\{\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\}
during algorithm proceeding:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\leq\frac{1}{B}\left(2\eta_{x}B^{2}G^{2}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}\left(D^{2}+\eta_{y}\ell D^{2}\right)+2(3\eta_{x}G^{2}+D)D\right).

Proof.

Let
S=(T+1)/B
, and we choose
s=jB
,
j=0,...,S
. Then by summing over
t
on the both side of Lemma
B.4
we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})=\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{j=0}^{S}\sum_{t=jB}^{(j+1)B-1}\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{j=0}^{S}\left[2\eta_{x}B^{2}G^{2}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}\left(\|\boldsymbol{y}_{jB-1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(x_{jB})\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{jB-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{jB-2}\|^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{j=0}^{S}\left(-\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{(j+1)B-1},y_{(j+1)B-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{(j+1)B-2},y_{(j+1)B-2}),\right.

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\left.\boldsymbol{y}_{(j+1)B-1}-y^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{jB})\rangle+\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{jB-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{jB-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{jB-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{jB-2}),\boldsymbol{y}_{jB-1}-y^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{jB}\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{j=0}^{S}\left(2\eta_{x}B^{2}G^{2}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}\left(D^{2}+\frac{1}{2}D^{2}\right)+2(3\eta_{x}G^{2}+D)D\right)

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{1}{B}\left(2\eta_{x}B^{2}G^{2}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}\left(D^{2}+\eta_{y}\ell D^{2}\right)+2(3\eta_{x}G^{2}+D)D\right).

∎

B.1.2
Proof of Theorem
4.8
for OGDA

In this section we are going to provide the proof of Theorem
4.8
on the convergence rate of OGDA in both deterministic and stochastic settings.

We start by establishing the convergence rate in deterministic setting. Before, we first state the formal version of Theorem
4.8
here:

Theorem B.6

(OGDA Deterministic (Theorem
4.8
restated))
.

Under Assumption
4.7
, if we choose
\eta_{x}=\Theta\left(\min\left\{\frac{\epsilon}{\ell G},\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\ell G^{2}},\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{D^{2}G^{2}\ell^{3}}\right\}\right)
,
\eta_{y}=\frac{1}{2\ell}
, then OGDA (Algorithm
2
) guarantees to find
\epsilon
-stationary point, i.e.,
\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}\leq\epsilon^{2}
, with the gradient complexity bounded by:

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\ell G^{2}\hat{\Delta}_{\Phi}}{\epsilon^{4}}\max\left\{1,\frac{D^{2}\ell^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right\}\right).

Proof.

From Lemma
B.2
we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Phi_{1/2\ell}({\boldsymbol{x}}_{0})-\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})}{\eta_{x}T}+16\ell\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(\Phi({\boldsymbol{x}}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\right)+24\eta_{x}\ell G^{2}

\displaystyle+4\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\|\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\|^{2},

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Phi_{1/2\ell}({\boldsymbol{x}}_{0})-\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})}{\eta_{x}T}+16\ell\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(\Phi({\boldsymbol{x}}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\right)+24\eta_{x}\ell G^{2}

\displaystyle+4\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\ell^{2}(3\eta_{x}^{2}G^{2}+\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}).

Plugging in Lemma
B.3
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}\leq\frac{\Phi_{1/2\ell}({\boldsymbol{x}}_{0})-\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})}{\eta_{x}T}

\displaystyle\qquad+16\ell\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\left(\Phi({\boldsymbol{x}}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\right)+24\eta_{x}\ell G^{2}+12\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}G^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+4\frac{1}{T+1}\ell^{2}\left(\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{j}\right)4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\right)+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})^{j}\right)2\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}G^{2}\right),

since we choose
\eta_{y}\ell\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we know that:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{T}\left(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\right)^{j}\leq 2.

Hence we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Phi_{1/2\ell}({\boldsymbol{x}}_{0})-\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})}{\eta_{x}T}+(16\ell+32\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{3})\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\Phi({\boldsymbol{x}}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+24\eta_{x}\ell G^{2}+12\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}G^{2}+16\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{4}G^{2}.

Now we plug in Lemma
B.5
to replace
\Phi({\boldsymbol{x}}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Phi_{1/2\ell}({\boldsymbol{x}}_{0})-\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})}{\eta_{x}T}

\displaystyle\quad+(16\ell+32\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{3})\frac{1}{B}\left(2\eta_{x}B^{2}G^{2}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}\left(D^{2}+\eta_{y}\ell D^{2}\right)+2(3\eta_{x}G^{2}+D)D\right)

\displaystyle\quad+24\eta_{x}\ell G^{2}+12\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}G^{2}+16\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{4}G^{2}.

Choose
B=O\left(\frac{D}{G\sqrt{\eta_{x}\eta_{y}}}\right),\eta_{x}=O\left(\min\left\{\frac{\epsilon}{\ell G},\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\ell G^{2}},\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{D^{2}G^{2}\ell^{3}}\right\}\right)
,
\eta_{y}=\frac{1}{2\ell}
, and then we guarantee that
\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}\leq\epsilon^{2}
with the gradient complexity is bounded by:

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{\ell G^{2}\hat{\Delta}_{\Phi}}{\epsilon^{4}}\max\left\{1,\frac{D^{2}\ell^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right\}\right).

∎

Stochastic setting.

We now turn to presenting the proof of OGDA in stochastic setting. First let us introduce some useful lemmas.

B.1.3
Useful Lemmas

Lemma B.7
.

For Stochastic OGDA (Algorithm
2
), under the same assumptions made in Theorem
4.9
, if we choose
\eta\leq 1/4\ell
the following statement holds for the generated sequence
\{\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\}
during algorithm proceeding and for any
\boldsymbol{y}\in\mathcal{Y}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle{\leq}\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\|^{2}+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle

\displaystyle+\eta_{y}\eta_{x}^{2}\ell(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}-2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\rangle.

Proof.

The proof is similar to deterministic setting. Here we use
\xi_{t-1}
to denote the random sample at iteration
t
. According to updating rule of
\boldsymbol{y}
:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}=\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}+2\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1};\xi_{t-1})-\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2};\xi_{t-1})\right)

Similarly to deterministic setting, we let

\displaystyle\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}=\eta_{y}(\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1};\xi_{t-1}))-\eta_{y}(\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1};\xi_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2};\xi_{t-1}))

\displaystyle{\varepsilon}_{t-1}=\eta_{y}(\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}))-\eta_{y}(\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}))

and re-write the updating rule as:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}=\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left(\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}+\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\right)

Due to the property of projection we have:

\displaystyle(\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t})^{\top}(\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})+\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1})\geq 0

Using the identity that
\langle\boldsymbol{a},\boldsymbol{b}\rangle=\frac{1}{2}(\|\boldsymbol{a}+\boldsymbol{b}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{a}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{b}\|^{2})
we have:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})+\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})+\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+2\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle

\displaystyle+2\langle\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1},\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle-2\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1},\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle.

Notice that

\displaystyle-2\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1},\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle=-2\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1},{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle-2\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1},\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}-{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq-2\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1},{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+2\|\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}-{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\|^{2}

So we have:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})+\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})+\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+2\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+2\langle\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1},\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle-2\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1},{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+2\|\tilde{\varepsilon}_{t-1}-{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\|^{2}.

Taking expectation over
\xi_{t-1}
yields:

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+2\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\eta_{y}\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-2\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},{\varepsilon}_{t-1}\rangle+6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}.

Now we plug the definition of
\varepsilon_{t-1}
into above inequality:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\rangle+6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\rangle+6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\eta_{y}\ell(\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2}\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\|^{2})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{➀}}}{{\leq}}\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle+\eta_{y}\eta_{x}^{2}\ell(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+2\eta_{y}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\rangle,

where in
➀
we use the fact that
\eta_{y}\ell\leq\frac{1}{4}
and hence can conclude the proof.

∎

Lemma B.8
.

For Stochastic OGDA (Algorithm
2
), under same assumptions as in Theorem
4.9
, the following statement holds for the genserated sequence
\{\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\},\{\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\}
during algorithm proceeding:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2\ell}({\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1})]+2\eta_{x}\ell\mathbb{E}\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\right)-\frac{\eta_{x}}{8}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+3\ell\eta_{x}^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\|^{2}.

Proof.

Let
\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}=\arg\min_{\boldsymbol{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\Phi(\boldsymbol{x})+\ell\|\boldsymbol{x}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\|^{2}
. Notice that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1})]+\ell\mathbb{E}\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1})]

\displaystyle\quad+\ell(\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}-\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}\|^{2}+2\eta_{x}\langle 2\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}),\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}-\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+3\eta_{x}^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2}))

According to smoothness of
f(\cdot,\boldsymbol{y})
, we have:

\displaystyle\langle\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1},\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\rangle

\displaystyle\leq f(\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})+\frac{\ell}{2}\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\frac{\ell}{2}\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\|^{2}.

So we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1})]+\ell\mathbb{E}\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1})]+\ell\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}-\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+2\eta_{x}\ell\mathbb{E}\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\frac{\ell}{2}\mathbb{E}\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\|^{2}\right)+3\ell\eta_{x}^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\eta_{x}\ell\left(\frac{1}{2\ell}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\|^{2}+\frac{\ell}{2}\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}-\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{1/2\ell}({\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1})]+2\eta_{x}\ell\mathbb{E}\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\right)-\frac{\eta_{x}\ell^{2}}{2}\mathbb{E}\|\hat{\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+3\ell\eta_{x}^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+\frac{\eta_{x}}{2}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{x}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\|^{2}.

∎

Lemma B.9
.

For Stochastic OGDA (Algorithm
2
), under Theorem
4.9
’s assumptions, the following statement holds for the generated sequence
\{\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\}
during algorithm proceeding:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}\left(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\right)^{j}\right)\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}\left(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\right)^{j}\right)\left(6\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+{6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}}\right)

Proof.

According to updating rule of stochastic OGDA:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\eta_{y}^{2}\mathbb{E}\|2\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1};\xi_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2};\xi_{t-1})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\eta_{y}^{2}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})\|^{2}+2\eta_{y}^{2}{\sigma^{2}}+2\eta_{y}^{2}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2})\|^{2}+4\eta_{y}^{2}{\sigma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})]+2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}(\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2}\|^{2}+\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\|^{2})+{6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})]+2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}(3\eta_{x}^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\|^{2})+{6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}}.

Unrolling the recursion yields:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\right)^{t-1-j}\mathbb{E}[\Phi(x_{j})-f(x_{j},y_{j})]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=0}^{t-1}\left(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\right)^{t-1-j}\left(6\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+{6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}}\right)+(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2})\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{0}-\boldsymbol{y}_{-1}\|^{2}.

Since
\boldsymbol{y}_{0}=\boldsymbol{y}_{-1}
, we can conclude the proof via summing
t
from
0
to
T-1
:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}\left(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\right)^{j}\right)\mathbb{E}[\Phi(x_{t})-f(x_{t},y_{t})]

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}\left(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\right)^{j}\right)\left(6\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+{6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}}\right).

∎

Lemma B.10
.

For Stochastic OGDA (Algorithm
2
), under assumptions made in Theorem
4.9
, the following statement holds for the generated sequence
\{\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\}
during algorithm proceeding and
\forall s\leq t
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})]\leq 2\eta_{x}(t-s)G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+\frac{\eta_{y}\eta_{x}^{2}\ell}{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+3\eta_{y}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}\left(\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\|^{2}-\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\|^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\mathbb{E}\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}),\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{t-2}),\boldsymbol{y}_{t-1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\rangle.

Proof.

Observe that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}[f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t}))-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t}))]+\mathbb{E}[f(\boldsymbol{x}_{s},\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s}))-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s}))]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}[f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s}))-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})]

\displaystyle\leq 2(t-s)\eta_{x}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}-\mathbb{E}\langle\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y},\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\rangle.

Plugging in Lemma
B.7
will conclude the proof.
∎

Lemma B.11
.

For Stochastic OGDA (Algorithm
2
), under Theorem
4.9
’s assumptions, the following statement holds for the generated sequence
\{\boldsymbol{x}_{t}\},\{\boldsymbol{y}_{t}\}
during algorithm proceeding:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{B}\left(2\eta_{x}B^{2}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+\frac{5D^{2}}{8\eta_{y}}+2(3\eta_{x}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+D)D\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{y}\eta_{x}^{2}\ell}{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+3\eta_{y}\sigma^{2}.

Proof.

Let
S=(T+1)/B
, and we choose
s=jB
,
j=0,...,S
. Then by summing over
t
on the both side of Lemma
B.11
we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})]=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{j=0}^{S}\sum_{t=jB}^{(j+1)B-1}\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{j=0}^{S}\left[2\eta_{x}B^{2}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}\left(\|y_{jB}-y^{*}(x_{jB})\|^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\|y_{jB}-y_{jB-1}\|^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{y}\eta_{x}^{2}\ell}{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+3\eta_{y}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{j=0}^{S}\left[-\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{(j+1)B-1},y_{(j+1)B-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{(j+1)B-2},y_{(j+1)B-2}),\boldsymbol{y}_{(j+1)B-1}-y^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{jB})\rangle\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+\langle\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{jB-1},\boldsymbol{y}_{jB-1})-\nabla_{y}f(\boldsymbol{x}_{jB-2},\boldsymbol{y}_{jB-2}),\boldsymbol{y}_{jB-1}-y^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{jB}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{j=0}^{S}\left[2\eta_{x}B^{2}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+\frac{5D^{2}}{8\eta_{y}}+2(3\eta_{x}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+D)D\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{y}\eta_{x}^{2}\ell}{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+3\eta_{y}\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{B}\left[2\eta_{x}B^{2}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+\frac{5D^{2}}{8\eta_{y}}+2(3\eta_{x}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+D)D\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta_{y}\eta_{x}^{2}\ell}{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+3\eta_{y}\sigma^{2}.

∎

B.1.4
Proof of Theorem
4.9
for OGDA

In this section we are going to provide the proof for Theorem
4.9
, the convergence rate of OGDA in stochastic setting. We first introduce the formal version of Theorem
4.9
here:

Theorem B.12

(OGDA Stochastic (Theorem
4.9
restated))
.

Under Assumption
4.3
and
4.7
, if we choose
\eta_{x}=O(\min\{\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\ell(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})},\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{D^{2}\ell^{3}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}},\frac{\epsilon^{6}}{D^{2}\ell^{3}\sigma^{2}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}}\})
,
\eta_{y}=O(\min\{\frac{1}{4\ell},\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\ell\sigma^{2}}\})
, then Stochastic OGDA (Algorithm
2
) guarantees to find
\epsilon
-stationary point, i.e.,
\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}\leq\epsilon^{2}
, with the gradient complexity bounded by:

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{D^{2}\ell^{3}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}}{\epsilon^{6}}\max\left\{1,\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right\}\right).

Proof.

Similar to the proof in deterministic setting, first according to Lemma
B.8
we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{0})-\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{T+1})}{\eta_{x}(T+1)}

\displaystyle\quad+16\ell\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\right)+12\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+24\ell\eta_{x}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+4\ell^{2}\frac{1}{T+1}\left(4\eta_{y}^{2}\ell\sum_{t=0}^{T+1}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}\left(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\right)^{j}\right)\mathbb{E}[\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})]\right.

\displaystyle\quad+\left.\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\sum_{j=0}^{T}\left(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\right)^{j}\right)\left(6\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}\right)\right).

Since we choose
\eta_{y}\ell\leq\frac{1}{4}
, it follows that:

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{T}\left(2\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}\right)^{j}\leq 2.

As a result, we can further simplify the bound as:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Phi_{1/2\ell}({x}_{0})-\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{T+1})}{\eta_{x}(T+1)}

\displaystyle\quad+(16\ell+32\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{3})\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\left(\Phi({x}_{t})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t},\boldsymbol{y}_{t})\right)

\displaystyle\quad+12\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+24\ell\eta_{x}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+8\ell^{2}\left(6\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+{6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}}\right).

Plugging in Lemma
B.11
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Phi_{1/2\ell}({x}_{0})-\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{T+1})}{\eta_{x}(T+1)}

\displaystyle\quad+(16\ell+32\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{3})\frac{1}{B}\left(2\eta_{x}B^{2}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+\frac{5D^{2}}{8\eta_{y}}+2(3\eta_{x}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}+D)D\right)

\displaystyle\quad+(16\ell+32\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{3})(\frac{\eta_{y}\eta_{x}^{2}\ell}{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+3\eta_{y}\sigma^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+12\eta_{x}^{2}\ell^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+24\ell\eta_{x}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+8\ell^{2}\left(6\eta_{x}^{2}\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{2}(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})+{6\eta_{y}^{2}\sigma^{2}}\right).

Choose
B=O(\frac{D}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}\eta_{y}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}}})
,
\eta_{x}=O(\min\{\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\ell(G^{2}+\sigma^{2})},\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{D^{2}\ell^{3}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}},\frac{\epsilon^{6}}{D^{2}\ell^{3}\sigma^{2}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}}\})
,
\eta_{y}=O(\min\{\frac{1}{4\ell},\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{\ell\sigma^{2}}\})
, and then it is guaranteed that
\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\mathbb{E}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{t})\|^{2}\leq\epsilon^{2}
with the gradient complexity is bounded by

\displaystyle O\left(\frac{D^{2}\ell^{3}G\sqrt{G^{2}+\sigma^{2}}}{\epsilon^{6}}\max\left\{1,\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\epsilon^{2}}\right\}\right).

∎

B.2
Proof of convergence of EG

In this section, the convergence of EG in NC-C setting has been established. Before presenting the complete proofs, here we briefly discuss the proof sketch.

Proof sketch

Similar to OGDA, we have the following lemma on
\Phi_{1/2\ell}
:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\|\nabla\Phi_{1/2\ell}({\boldsymbol{x}}_{t-\frac{1}{2}})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Phi_{1/2\ell}({\boldsymbol{x}}_{-\frac{1}{2}})-\Phi_{1/2\ell}(\boldsymbol{x}_{T+\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle\quad+O(\ell+\eta_{y}^{2}\ell^{3})\frac{1}{T+1}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\delta_{t-\frac{1}{2}}+O(\ell\eta_{x}^{2}G^{2}).

Now we need to examine
\delta_{t-\frac{1}{2}}
. To bound this term, we have the following recursion:

\displaystyle\Phi(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}})-f(\boldsymbol{x}_{t+\frac{1}{2}},\boldsymbol{y}_{t+\frac{1}{2}})

\displaystyle\leq O((t-s)\eta_{x}G^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2\eta_{y}}\left(\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\|^{2}-\|\boldsymbol{y}_{t+1}-\boldsymbol{y}^{*}(\boldsymbol{x}_{s})\|^{2}+\frac{\eta_{x}^{2}G^{2}}{2}\right),

which is derived by the descent property of EG on concave function.
Similar to OGDA, here we also obtain neat recursion, which will yield our desired complexity bound.

In the following, we present the key lemmas, and complete convergence proof of EG. First let us introduce some useful lemmas for the deterministic setting.

B.2.1
Useful Lemmas

Proposition B.13

(
[
5
]
, Proposition 4.2)
.

If
\boldsymbol{p}=\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}(\boldsymbol{r}-\boldsymbol{u})
,
\boldsymbol{q}=\mathcal{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}(\boldsymbol{r}-\boldsymbol{v})
, and
